            EXPORTING
              is_data = mainactchangeset_update_entity
            CHANGING
              cr_data = er_entity
          ).
        ELSE.
*         In case of initial values - unbind the entity reference
          er_entity = lr_entity.
        ENDIF.

    ).
 ENDCASE.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~COMMIT_WORK
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_RFC_DEST                    TYPE        /IWBEP/DEFI_RFC_DEST(optional)
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~COMMIT_WORK.
* Call RFC commit work functionality
DATA lt_message      TYPE bapiret2. "#EC NEEDED
DATA lv_message_text TYPE BAPI_MSG.
DATA lo_logger       TYPE REF TO /iwbep/cl_cos_logger.
DATA lv_subrc        TYPE syst-subrc.

lo_logger = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_logger( ).

  IF iv_rfc_dest IS INITIAL OR iv_rfc_dest EQ 'NONE'.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      EXPORTING
      wait   = abap_true
    IMPORTING
      return = lt_message.
  ELSE.
    CALL FUNCTION 'BAPI_TRANSACTION_COMMIT'
      DESTINATION iv_rfc_dest
    EXPORTING
      wait                  = abap_true
    IMPORTING
      return                = lt_message
    EXCEPTIONS
      communication_failure = 1000 MESSAGE lv_message_text
      system_failure        = 1001 MESSAGE lv_message_text
      OTHERS                = 1002.

  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
    /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>rfc_exception_handling(
        EXPORTING
          iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
          iv_exp_message_text = lv_message_text
          io_logger           = lo_logger ).
  ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~GET_GENERATION_STRATEGY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [<-()] RV_GENERATION_STRATEGY         TYPE        I
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~GET_GENERATION_STRATEGY.
* Get generation strategy
  rv_generation_strategy = '1'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->/IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~LOG_MESSAGE
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_MSG_TYPE                    TYPE        SYMSGTY
* | [--->] IV_MSG_ID                      TYPE        SYMSGID(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_NUMBER                  TYPE        SYMSGNO(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V1                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V2                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V3                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* | [--->] IV_MSG_V4                      TYPE        ANY(optional)
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_SB_DPC_COMM_SERVICES~LOG_MESSAGE.
* Log message in the application log
DATA lo_logger TYPE REF TO /iwbep/cl_cos_logger.
DATA lv_text TYPE /iwbep/sup_msg_longtext.

  MESSAGE ID iv_msg_id TYPE iv_msg_type NUMBER iv_msg_number
    WITH iv_msg_v1 iv_msg_v2 iv_msg_v3 iv_msg_v4 INTO lv_text.

  lo_logger = mo_context->get_logger( ).
  lo_logger->log_message(
    EXPORTING
     iv_msg_type   = iv_msg_type
     iv_msg_id     = iv_msg_id
     iv_msg_number = iv_msg_number
     iv_msg_text   = lv_text
     iv_msg_v1     = iv_msg_v1
     iv_msg_v2     = iv_msg_v2
     iv_msg_v3     = iv_msg_v3
     iv_msg_v4     = iv_msg_v4
     iv_agent      = 'DPC' ).
  endmethod.

  lo_logger = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_logger( ).
  lo_message_container = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_message_container( ).

  " Save the RFC call log in the application log

      is_return            = is_return

  endmethod.

* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_D(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PORTACCOMMODATIO_DELETE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PORTACCOMMODATIO_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.

* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

      method = 'PORTBERTHSET_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->PORTBERTHSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TS_PORTBERTH
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PORTBERTHSET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PORTBERTHSET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->PORTBERTHSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TT_PORTBERTH
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PORTBERTHSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_case TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>scmg_ext_key.
 DATA iv_port TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zde_ega_fshry_port.
 DATA iv_reserved_items TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>flag.
 DATA et_berth  TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zega_cj_epda_port_objects_tt.
 DATA ls_et_berth  TYPE LINE OF zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zega_cj_epda_port_objects_tt.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_case LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_case.
 DATA ls_iv_case LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_case.
 DATA lr_iv_port LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_port.
 DATA ls_iv_port LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_port.
 DATA lr_iv_reserved_items LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_reserved_items.
 DATA ls_iv_reserved_items LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_reserved_items.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_berth LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_CASE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_case ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_case INTO ls_iv_case INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_case = ls_iv_case-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_PORT'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_port ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_port INTO ls_iv_port INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_port = ls_iv_port-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_RESERVED_ITEMS'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_reserved_items ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_reserved_items INTO ls_iv_reserved_items INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_reserved_items = ls_iv_reserved_items-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZEGA_CJ_EPDA_PORT_OBJECTS'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_case           = iv_case
           iv_port           = iv_port
           iv_reserved_items = iv_reserved_items
         IMPORTING
           et_berth          = et_berth
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure    = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS            = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_case               = iv_case
       iv_port               = iv_port
       iv_reserved_items     = iv_reserved_items
     IMPORTING
       et_berth              = et_berth
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_berth ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_berth INTO ls_et_berth
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_berth-available_to = ls_et_berth-available_to.
   ls_gw_et_berth-arch_object_id = ls_et_berth-arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_berth-arch_object_type = ls_et_berth-arch_object_type.
   ls_gw_et_berth-part_arch_object_id = ls_et_berth-part_arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_berth-arch_object_number = ls_et_berth-arch_object_number.
   ls_gw_et_berth-parent_arch_object_id = ls_et_berth-parent_arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_berth-arch_object_text = ls_et_berth-arch_object_text.
   ls_gw_et_berth-available = ls_et_berth-available.
   ls_gw_et_berth-available_from = ls_et_berth-available_from.
   ls_gw_et_berth-shared_storage = ls_et_berth-shared_storage.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_berth TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_berth.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PORTSTORAGESET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_case TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>scmg_ext_key.
 DATA iv_port TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zde_ega_fshry_port.
 DATA iv_reserved_items TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>flag.
 DATA et_storage  TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zega_cj_epda_port_objects_tt.
 DATA ls_et_storage  TYPE LINE OF zif_zega_cj_epda_port_objects=>zega_cj_epda_port_objects_tt.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_case LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_case.
 DATA ls_iv_case LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_case.
 DATA lr_iv_port LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_port.
 DATA ls_iv_port LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_port.
 DATA lr_iv_reserved_items LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_reserved_items.
 DATA ls_iv_reserved_items LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_reserved_items.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_storage LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_CASE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_case ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_case INTO ls_iv_case INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_case = ls_iv_case-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_PORT'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_port ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_port INTO ls_iv_port INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_port = ls_iv_port-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_RESERVED_ITEMS'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_reserved_items ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_reserved_items INTO ls_iv_reserved_items INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_reserved_items = ls_iv_reserved_items-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZEGA_CJ_EPDA_PORT_OBJECTS'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_case           = iv_case
           iv_port           = iv_port
           iv_reserved_items = iv_reserved_items
         IMPORTING
           et_storage        = et_storage
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure    = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS            = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_case               = iv_case
       iv_port               = iv_port
       iv_reserved_items     = iv_reserved_items
     IMPORTING
       et_storage            = et_storage
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_storage ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_storage INTO ls_et_storage
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_storage-arch_object_id = ls_et_storage-arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_storage-arch_object_type = ls_et_storage-arch_object_type.
   ls_gw_et_storage-part_arch_object_id = ls_et_storage-part_arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_storage-arch_object_number = ls_et_storage-arch_object_number.
   ls_gw_et_storage-parent_arch_object_id = ls_et_storage-parent_arch_object_id.
   ls_gw_et_storage-arch_object_text = ls_et_storage-arch_object_text.
   ls_gw_et_storage-available = ls_et_storage-available.
   ls_gw_et_storage-available_from = ls_et_storage-available_from.
   ls_gw_et_storage-available_to = ls_et_storage-available_to.
   ls_gw_et_storage-shared_storage = ls_et_storage-shared_storage.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_storage TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_storage.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.

* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT

  method PORTUPDATESET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'PORTUPDATESET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->PORTUPDATESET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TT_PORTUPDATE
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method PORTUPDATESET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_cancel TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_update_port_o=>flag.
 DATA iv_case TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_update_port_o=>scmg_ext_key.
 DATA et_messages  TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_update_port_o=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA ls_et_messages  TYPE LINE OF zif_zega_cj_epda_update_port_o=>bapiret2_t.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_case LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_case.
 DATA ls_iv_case LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_case.
 DATA lr_iv_cancel LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_cancel.
 DATA ls_iv_cancel LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_cancel.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_messages LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_CASE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_case ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_case INTO ls_iv_case INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_case = ls_iv_case-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN 'IV_CANCEL'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_cancel ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_cancel INTO ls_iv_cancel INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_cancel = ls_iv_cancel-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZEGA_CJ_EPDA_UPDATE_PORT_OBJ'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_case        = iv_case
           iv_cancel      = iv_cancel
         IMPORTING
           et_messages    = et_messages
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_case               = iv_case
       iv_cancel             = iv_cancel
     IMPORTING
       et_messages           = et_messages
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_messages ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_messages INTO ls_et_messages
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_messages-system = ls_et_messages-system.
   ls_gw_et_messages-field = ls_et_messages-field.
   ls_gw_et_messages-row = ls_et_messages-row.
   ls_gw_et_messages-parameter = ls_et_messages-parameter.
   ls_gw_et_messages-message_v4 = ls_et_messages-message_v4.
   ls_gw_et_messages-message_v3 = ls_et_messages-message_v3.
   ls_gw_et_messages-message_v2 = ls_et_messages-message_v2.
   ls_gw_et_messages-message_v1 = ls_et_messages-message_v1.
   ls_gw_et_messages-log_msg_no = ls_et_messages-log_msg_no.
   ls_gw_et_messages-log_no = ls_et_messages-log_no.
   ls_gw_et_messages-message = ls_et_messages-message.
   ls_gw_et_messages-number = ls_et_messages-number.
   ls_gw_et_messages-id = ls_et_messages-id.
   ls_gw_et_messages-type = ls_et_messages-type.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_messages TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_messages.
 ENDLOOP.

* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_D(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method TOKENSET_DELETE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'TOKENSET_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.

* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION

  method WAITINGLISTSET_DELETE_ENTITY.

      method = 'WAITINGLISTSET_DELETE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WAITINGLISTSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TS_WAITINGLIST
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WAITINGLISTSET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'WAITINGLISTSET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WAITINGLISTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TT_WAITINGLIST
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WAITINGLISTSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA dummy TYPE string.
 DATA et_waiting_list  TYPE zif_zfe_cj_get_waiting_list1=>ztt_cj_epda_waiting_list.
 DATA ls_et_waiting_list  TYPE LINE OF zif_zfe_cj_get_waiting_list1=>ztt_cj_epda_waiting_list.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_dummy LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-dummy.
 DATA ls_dummy LIKE LINE OF lr_dummy.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_waiting_list LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'DUMMY'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_dummy ).

         READ TABLE lr_dummy INTO ls_dummy INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           dummy = ls_dummy-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZFE_CJ_GET_WAITING_LIST'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           dummy           = dummy
         IMPORTING
           et_waiting_list = et_waiting_list
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure  = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS          = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       dummy                 = dummy
     IMPORTING
       et_waiting_list       = et_waiting_list
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_waiting_list ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_waiting_list INTO ls_et_waiting_list
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-serv_free = ls_et_waiting_list-serv_free.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-case_id = ls_et_waiting_list-case_id.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-case_type = ls_et_waiting_list-case_type.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-case_desc = ls_et_waiting_list-case_desc.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-application_number = ls_et_waiting_list-application_number.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-lic_no = ls_et_waiting_list-lic_no.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-port = ls_et_waiting_list-port.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-port_name = ls_et_waiting_list-port_name.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-submit_dt = ls_et_waiting_list-submit_dt.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-aotype = ls_et_waiting_list-aotype.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-xmaotype = ls_et_waiting_list-xmaotype.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-owner_id = ls_et_waiting_list-owner_id.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-owner_name = ls_et_waiting_list-owner_name.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-relat_mobile = ls_et_waiting_list-relat_mobile.
   ls_gw_et_waiting_list-relat_email = ls_et_waiting_list-relat_email.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_waiting_list TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_waiting_list.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WAITINGLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TS_WAITINGLIST
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WAITINGLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'WAITINGLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+

* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WORKERSLISTSET_GET_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_REQUEST_OBJECT              TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TS_WORKERSLIST
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WORKERSLISTSET_GET_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'WORKERSLISTSET_GET_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WORKERSLISTSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TT_WORKERSLIST
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WORKERSLISTSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Data declaration
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 DATA iv_license TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_labors_list1=>recnnumber.
 DATA et_workers  TYPE zif_zega_cj_epda_labors_list1=>ztt_cj_fishery_workers.
 DATA ls_et_workers  TYPE LINE OF zif_zega_cj_epda_labors_list1=>ztt_cj_fishery_workers.
 DATA lv_rfc_name TYPE tfdir-funcname.
 DATA lv_destination TYPE rfcdest.
 DATA lv_subrc TYPE syst-subrc.
 DATA lv_exc_msg TYPE /iwbep/mgw_bop_rfc_excep_text.
 DATA lx_root TYPE REF TO cx_root.
 DATA lo_filter TYPE  REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_req_filter.
 DATA lt_filter_select_options TYPE /iwbep/t_mgw_select_option.
 DATA lv_filter_str TYPE string.
 DATA ls_paging TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_paging.
 DATA ls_converted_keys LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA ls_filter TYPE /iwbep/s_mgw_select_option.
 DATA ls_filter_range TYPE /iwbep/s_cod_select_option.
 DATA lr_iv_license LIKE RANGE OF ls_converted_keys-iv_license.
 DATA ls_iv_license LIKE LINE OF lr_iv_license.
 DATA lo_dp_facade TYPE REF TO /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_facade.
 DATA ls_gw_et_workers LIKE LINE OF et_entityset.
 DATA lv_skip     TYPE int4.
 DATA lv_top      TYPE int4.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the runtime request to the RFC - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Get all input information from the technical request context object
* Since DPC works with internal property names and runtime API interface holds external property names
* the process needs to get the all needed input information from the technical request context object
* Get filter or select option information
 lo_filter = io_tech_request_context->get_filter( ).
 lt_filter_select_options = lo_filter->get_filter_select_options( ).
 lv_filter_str = lo_filter->get_filter_string( ).

* Check if the supplied filter is supported by standard gateway runtime process
 IF  lv_filter_str            IS NOT INITIAL
 AND lt_filter_select_options IS INITIAL.
   " If the string of the Filter System Query Option is not automatically converted into
   " filter option table (lt_filter_select_options), then the filtering combination is not supported
   " Log message in the application log
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
     EXPORTING
       iv_msg_type   = 'E'
       iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
       iv_msg_number = 025 ).
   " Raise Exception
   RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
     EXPORTING
       textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
 ENDIF.

* Get key table information
 io_tech_request_context->get_converted_source_keys(
   IMPORTING
     es_key_values  = ls_converted_keys ).

 ls_paging-top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).
 ls_paging-skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).

* Maps filter table lines to function module parameters
 LOOP AT lt_filter_select_options INTO ls_filter.

   LOOP AT ls_filter-select_options INTO ls_filter_range.
     CASE ls_filter-property.
       WHEN 'IV_LICENSE'.
         lo_filter->convert_select_option(
           EXPORTING
             is_select_option = ls_filter
           IMPORTING
             et_select_option = lr_iv_license ).

         READ TABLE lr_iv_license INTO ls_iv_license INDEX 1.
         IF sy-subrc = 0.
           iv_license = ls_iv_license-low.
         ENDIF.
       WHEN OTHERS.
         " Log message in the application log
         me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~log_message(
           EXPORTING
             iv_msg_type   = 'E'
             iv_msg_id     = '/IWBEP/MC_SB_DPC_ADM'
             iv_msg_number = 020
             iv_msg_v1     = ls_filter-property ).
         " Raise Exception
         RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception
           EXPORTING
             textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception=>internal_error.
     ENDCASE.
   ENDLOOP.

 ENDLOOP.

* Get RFC destination
 lo_dp_facade = /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
 lv_destination = /iwbep/cl_sb_gen_dpc_rt_util=>get_rfc_destination( io_dp_facade = lo_dp_facade ).

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Call RFC function module
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 lv_rfc_name = 'ZEGA_CJ_EPDA_LABORS_LIST'.

 IF lv_destination IS INITIAL OR lv_destination EQ 'NONE'.

   TRY.
       CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name
         EXPORTING
           iv_license     = iv_license
         IMPORTING
           et_workers     = et_workers
         EXCEPTIONS
           system_failure = 1000 message lv_exc_msg
           OTHERS         = 1002.

       lv_subrc = sy-subrc.
*in case of co-deployment the exception is raised and needs to be caught
     CATCH cx_root INTO lx_root.
       lv_subrc = 1001.
       lv_exc_msg = lx_root->if_message~get_text( ).
   ENDTRY.

 ELSE.

   CALL FUNCTION lv_rfc_name DESTINATION lv_destination
     EXPORTING
       iv_license            = iv_license
     IMPORTING
       et_workers            = et_workers
     EXCEPTIONS
       system_failure        = 1000 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       communication_failure = 1001 MESSAGE lv_exc_msg
       OTHERS                = 1002.

   lv_subrc = sy-subrc.

 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------
*  Map the RFC response to the caller interface - Only mapped attributes
*-------------------------------------------------------------
*-------------------------------------------------------------
* Error and exception handling
*-------------------------------------------------------------
 IF lv_subrc <> 0.
* Execute the RFC exception handling process
   me->/iwbep/if_sb_dpc_comm_services~rfc_exception_handling(
     EXPORTING
       iv_subrc            = lv_subrc
       iv_exp_message_text = lv_exc_msg ).
 ENDIF.

*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
*             - Post Backend Call -
*-------------------------------------------------------------------------*
 IF ls_paging-skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  If the Skip value was requested at runtime
*  the response table will provide backend entries from skip + 1, meaning start from skip +1
*  for example: skip=5 means to start get results from the 6th row
   lv_skip = ls_paging-skip + 1.
 ENDIF.
*  The Top value was requested at runtime but was not handled as part of the function interface
 IF  ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND lv_skip IS NOT INITIAL.
*  if lv_skip > 0 retrieve the entries from lv_skip + Top - 1
*  for example: skip=5 and top=2 means to start get results from the 6th row and end in row number 7
   lv_top = ls_paging-top + lv_skip - 1.
 ELSEIF ls_paging-top <> 0
 AND    lv_skip IS INITIAL.
   lv_top = ls_paging-top.
 ELSE.
   lv_top = lines( et_workers ).
 ENDIF.

*  - Map properties from the backend to the Gateway output response table -

 LOOP AT et_workers INTO ls_et_workers
*  Provide the response entries according to the Top and Skip parameters that were provided at runtime
      FROM lv_skip TO lv_top.
*  Only fields that were mapped will be delivered to the response table
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer_to = ls_et_workers-transfer_to.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer_from = ls_et_workers-transfer_from.
   ls_gw_et_workers-nationality_tx = ls_et_workers-nationality_tx.
   ls_gw_et_workers-nationality = ls_et_workers-nationality.
   ls_gw_et_workers-email_address = ls_et_workers-email_address.
   ls_gw_et_workers-mobile_number = ls_et_workers-mobile_number.
   ls_gw_et_workers-id_expiry_on = ls_et_workers-id_expiry_on.
   ls_gw_et_workers-visa_id = ls_et_workers-visa_id.
   ls_gw_et_workers-emirates_id = ls_et_workers-emirates_id.
   ls_gw_et_workers-bp_name = ls_et_workers-bp_name.
   ls_gw_et_workers-room_allocat_tx = ls_et_workers-room_allocat_tx.
   ls_gw_et_workers-boat_name = ls_et_workers-boat_name.
   ls_gw_et_workers-visa_status_tx = ls_et_workers-visa_status_tx.
   ls_gw_et_workers-worker_status_tx = ls_et_workers-worker_status_tx.
   ls_gw_et_workers-changed_time = ls_et_workers-changed_time.
   ls_gw_et_workers-changed_on = ls_et_workers-changed_on.
   ls_gw_et_workers-created_time = ls_et_workers-created_time.
   ls_gw_et_workers-created_date = ls_et_workers-created_date.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer_end = ls_et_workers-transfer_end.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer_beg = ls_et_workers-transfer_beg.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer_licno = ls_et_workers-transfer_licno.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transferred = ls_et_workers-transferred.
   ls_gw_et_workers-transfer = ls_et_workers-transfer.
   ls_gw_et_workers-renew = ls_et_workers-renew.
   ls_gw_et_workers-worker_id_old = ls_et_workers-worker_id_old.
   ls_gw_et_workers-card_print = ls_et_workers-card_print.
   ls_gw_et_workers-card_exp_on = ls_et_workers-card_exp_on.
   ls_gw_et_workers-room_no = ls_et_workers-room_no.
   ls_gw_et_workers-room_allocat = ls_et_workers-room_allocat.
   ls_gw_et_workers-boat_id = ls_et_workers-boat_id.
   ls_gw_et_workers-visa_status = ls_et_workers-visa_status.
   ls_gw_et_workers-worker_status = ls_et_workers-worker_status.
   ls_gw_et_workers-worker_bp = ls_et_workers-worker_bp.
   ls_gw_et_workers-worker_id = ls_et_workers-worker_id.
   APPEND ls_gw_et_workers TO et_entityset.
   CLEAR ls_gw_et_workers.
 ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_DPC->WORKERSLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_U(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZEGA_EPDA_MAPLET_I_MPC=>TS_WORKERSLIST
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WORKERSLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY.
  RAISE EXCEPTION TYPE /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc
    EXPORTING
      textid = /iwbep/cx_mgw_not_impl_exc=>method_not_implemented
      method = 'WORKERSLISTSET_UPDATE_ENTITY'.
  endmethod.
ENDCLASS.
